Final rating = every learned contribution added together, shown in kg-equivalent (a common scale: 1 unit = the score difference that 1 kg of opposition-aware rating is worth on average); it is relative to the average runner in the race and the breakdown adds up to it exactly. Selection strength = how often top picks like this won in 2025–26 races the model was not fitted on (calibrated, accounts for field size). Labels are thirds of those historical races, not betting thresholds. "v1" columns show yesterday's model for comparison.
